Section 6 (Technical Specification) shows the technology stack:

Frontend:
Framework: React 18+ with TypeScript
Build Tool: Vite
State Management: Zustand and Redux Toolkit
Routing: React Router v6
UI Components: Custom components + Radix UI primitives
Styling: Tailwind CSS + CSS Modules
3D Graphics: WebGPU + Three.js (WebGPU backend)
Forms: React Hook Form + Zod validation
Data Fetching: TanStack Query (React Query)
Testing: Vitest, React Testing Library, and Playwright for end-to-end coverage
Additional utilities include Recharts and Chart.js for visualizations, date-fns for date handling, Lucide React for icons, and dnd-kit for drag-and-drop functionality

For a static site, I'm leaning toward either a client-side export button using jsPDF or html2canvas, or generating all 55 PDFs server-side with weasyprint and hosting them. cat << 'PYEOF' > /tmp/update_chapter_map.py import re filepath = '/home/sandbox/chapter_map_phases.md' with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: content = f.read() # Status and Dependencies data per phase row index phase_data = [ # (status, dependencies) ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "— (จุดเริ่มต้น)"), # Foundation ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Foundation"), # Phase 1 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 1"), # Phase 2 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 2"), # Phase 3 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 3"), # Phase 4 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 4"), # Phase 5 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 5"), # Phase 6 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 5, Phase 6"), # Phase 7 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 6, Phase 7"), # Phase 8 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 8"), # Phase 9 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 9"), # Phase 10 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 10"), # Phase 11 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 7, Phase 11"), # Phase 12 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 6, Phase 12"), # Phase 13 ("✅ สมบูรณ์", "Phase 13"), # Phase 14 ("🔄 กำลังดำเนินการ", "Phase 14 + ทุก Phase ก่อนหน้า"), # Phase 15 ] # Find the main table section and replace header + rows old_header = "| # | Phase | ชื่อ Phase | Chapters หลัก | MCP Tools (ใหม่) | รวมสะสม | Key Modules |" new_header = "| # | Phase | ชื่อ Phase | Chapters หลัก | MCP Tools (ใหม่) | รวมสะสม | Key Modules | Status | Dependencies |" old_sep = "|---|-------|-----------|---------------|:----------------:|:-------:|-------------|" new_sep = "|---|-------|-----------|---------------|:----------------:|:-------:|-------------|:------:|-------------|" content = content.replace(old_header, new_header) content = content.replace(old_sep, new_sep) # Now update each data row — match rows starting with | 0 | ** or | 1 | ** etc. lines = content.split('\n') new_lines = [] row_index = 0 in_main_table = False for line in lines: if line == new_header: in_main_table = True new_lines.append(line) continue if in_main_table and line.startswith('|') and not line.startswith('|---|') and not line.startswith('| กลุ่ม') and not line.startswith('| จุด'): # This is a data row of the main table if row_index < len(phase_data): status, deps = phase_data[row_index] # Remove trailing | and add new columns stripped = line.rstrip() if stripped.endswith('|'): stripped = stripped[:-1] # remove last | new_line = stripped + f' | {status} | {deps} |' new_lines.append(new_line) row_index += 1 else: new_lines.append(line) continue if in_main_table and not line.startswith('|'): in_main_table = False new_lines.append(line) with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('\n'.join(new_lines)) print(f"Updated {row_index} rows. File saved.") PYEOF python3 /tmp/update_chapter_map.py

ได้ข้อมูลครบแล้ว เริ่ม implement ทั้ง 3 tasks ตามลำดับ

Step 1: สร้าง search_index.json

Command
cat > /home/sandbox/build_search_index.py << 'EOF' import json, re, html with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/chapters.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: chapters = json.load(f) def strip_html(text): text = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', text) text = html.unescape(text) text = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text).strip() return text index = [] for num_str, content in chapters.items(): plain = strip_html(content) index.append({"num": int(num_str), "text": plain[:6000]}) index.sort(key=lambda x: x["num"]) with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/search_index.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(index, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) total = sum(len(x["text"]) for x in index) print(f"Generated {len(index)} chapters, total chars: {total:,}") import os sz = os.path.getsize('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/search_index.json') print(f"File size: {sz/1024:.1f} KB") EOF python3 /home/sandbox/build_search_index.py

class="search-count"></div> <div id="search-results" class="search-results-list"></div> <div class="search-footer">กด <kbd>Esc</kbd> เพื่อปิด · คลิก Chapter เพื่อเปิด</div> </div> </div>''' html = encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(html) print("index.html patched:", len(html.splitlines()), "lines") # ========================================================= # 2. PATCH script.js # ========================================================= with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/script.js', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: js = f.read() # Add initTheme() call at start of DOMContentLoaded old_dcl = "document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {\n // Check URL hash" new_dcl = "document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {\n initTheme();\n // Check URL hash" js = js.replace(old_dcl, new_dcl, 1) # Append new functions new_functions = ''' // ─── Theme Toggle ────────────────────────────────────────── function initTheme() { const saved = localStorage.getItem('monolith-theme'); if (saved === 'light') { document.documentElement.classList.add('light'); const btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle'); if (btn) btn.textContent = '☀️'; } } function toggleTheme() { const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.toggle('light'); localStorage.setItem('monolith-theme', isLight ? 'light' : 'dark'); const btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle'); if (btn) btn.textContent = isLight ? '☀️' : '🌙'; } // ─── Full-text Search ────────────────────────────────────── let searchIndex = null; let searchDebounceTimer = null; async function loadSearchIndex() { if (searchIndex) return searchIndex; const res = await fetch('search_index.json'); searchIndex = await res.json(); return searchIndex; } function openSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.add('active'); setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('search-input').focus(), 60); loadSearchIndex(); // preload lazily } function closeSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('search-input').value = ''; document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; } function getSnippet(text, query, len) { len = len || 200; const lower = text.toLowerCase(); const idx = lower.indexOf(query.toLowerCase()); if (idx === -1) return text.substring(0, len) + '…'; const start = Math.max(0, idx - 70); const end = Math.min(text.length, idx + query.length + 130); const snippet = (start > 0 ? '…' : '') + text.substring(start, end) + (end < text.length ? '…' : ''); const re = new RegExp('(' + query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&') + ')', 'gi'); return snippet.replace(re, '<mark>$1</mark>'); } async function performSearch(query) { const q = query.trim(); if (q.length < 2) { document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; return; } const data = await loadSearchIndex(); const lower = q.toLowerCase(); const matches = []; for (const ch of data) { if (ch.text.toLowerCase().includes(lower)) { matches.push(ch); if (matches.length >= 20) break; } } const count = matches.length; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = count > 0 ? 'พบ ' + count + ' chapters' + (count >= 20 ? ' (แสดง 20 อันดับแรก)' : '') : ''; if (count === 0) { document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '<div class="search-no-results">ไม่พบผลลัพธ์สำหรับ "' + q + '"</div>'; return; } let html = ''; for (const ch of matches) { const m = CHAPTERS_META.find(function(x) { return x.num === ch.num; }); const title = m ? m.title : 'Chapter ' + ch.num; const snippet = getSnippet(ch.text, q); html += '<div class="search-result-item" onclick="showChapter(' + ch.num + '); closeSearch();">' + '<div class="sri-header"><span class="sri-num">Ch ' + ch.num + '</span>' + '<span class="sri-title">' + title + '</span></div>' + '<div class="sri-snippet">' + snippet + '</div></div>'; } document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = html; } function debouncedSearch(value) { clearTimeout(searchDebounceTimer); searchDebounceTimer = setTimeout(function() { performSearch(value); }, 250); } document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); openSearch(); } if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeSearch(); } }); ''' js = js + new_functions with open('/home/sandbox/monolith_site/script.js', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(js) print("script.js patched:", len(js.splitlines()), "lines") # ========================================================= # 3. APPEND to style.css # ========================================================= new_css = ''' /* ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── LIGHT MODE (html.light overrides CSS custom properties) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ html.light { --bg: #f6f8fa; --sidebar-bg: #ffffff; --border: #d0d7de; --text: #1f2328; --text-muted: #656d76; --accent: #0969da; --header-bg: #ffffff; --code-bg: #f0f2f5; --card-bg: #ffffff; --card-border: #d0d7de; --hover-bg: #f3f4f6; --link: #0969da; } html.light body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); } html.light #top-header { background: var(--header-bg); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.08); } html.light #sidebar { background: var(--sidebar-bg); border-right: 1px solid var(--border); } html.light .header-title { color: var(--text); } html.light .header-meta { color: var(--text-muted); } html.light .version-badge { background: #ddf4ff; color: var(--accent); border-left-color: var(--accent); } html.light .group-label { color: var(--text-muted); } html.light .sidebar-footer { color: var(--text-muted); border-top: 1px solid var(--border); } html.light .phase-card { background: var(--card-bg); border: 1px solid var(--card-border); } html.light .phase-card:hover { border-color: var(--accent); box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(9,105,218,0.12); } html.light .welcome-banner { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ddf4ff 0%, #f0f8ff 100%); border: 1px solid #b6e3ff; } html.light .welcome-banner h1 { color: var(--text); } html.light .badge { background: #ddf4ff; color: #0969da; border: 1px solid #b6e3ff; } html.light h2, html.light h3, html.light h4 { color: var(--text); } html.light p, html.light li { color: var(--text); } html.light code:not(.hljs) { background: var(--code-bg); color: #953800; } html.light pre { background: var(--code-bg) !important; } html.light .chapter-toolbar { background: var(--card-bg); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); } html.light .chapter-breadcrumb { color: var(--text-muted); } html.light .pdf-btn { background: #ddf4ff; color: #0969da; border: 1px solid #b6e3ff; } html.light .pdf-btn:hover { background: #b6e3ff; } html.light .chapter-nav a { background: var(--card-bg); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text); } html.light .chapter-nav a:hover { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); } html.light table { border-color: var(--border); } html.light th { background: #f0f2f5; color: var(--text); } html.light .loading-spinner { border-top-color: var(--accent); } /* ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── SEARCH OVERLAY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ .search-overlay { display: none; position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.65); backdrop-filter: blur(4px); z-index: 1000; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: center; padding-top: 80px; } .search-overlay.active { display: flex; } .search-modal { background: #1c1f26; border: 1px solid #30363d; border-radius: 12px; width: min(680px, 92vw); max-height: 70vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 24px 64px rgba(0,0,0,0.5); } html.light .search-modal { background: #ffffff; border-color: #d0d7de; box-shadow: 0 24px 64px rgba(0,0,0,0.18); } .search-input-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 14px 16px; outline: none; font-size: 16px; color: #e6edf3; font-family: inherit; } html.light #search-input { color: #1f2328; } #search-input::placeholder { color: #8b949e; } .search-close-btn { background: none; border: 1px solid #30363d; color: #8b949e; width: 26px; height: 26px; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1; flex-shrink: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; } .search-close-btn:hover { background: #30363d; color: #e6edf3; } html.light .search-close-btn { border-color: #d0d7de; color: #656d76; } html.light .search-close-btn:hover { background: #f0f2f5; color: #1f2328; } .search-count { padding: 6px 16px 0; font-size: 12px; color: #8b949e; min-height: 20px; } .search-results-list { overflow-y: auto; flex: 1; padding: 4px 8px 8px; } .search-result-item { padding: 12px; } .sri-header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 5px; } .sri-num { background: #f78166; color: #fff; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 7px; border-radius: 4px; flex-shrink: 0; } .sri-title { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; color: #e6edf3; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; } html.light .sri-title { color: #1f2328; } .sri-snippet { font-size: 12px; } .search-open-btn { padding: 5px 8px; } .header-meta { display: none; } } /* ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── CHAPTER MAP TABLE (Home Page) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ .home-section { margin-top: 36px; For a static site, localStorage is the most practical option—I can track chapter views per browser session and build an analytics dashboard page that reads from localStorage to display visualizations. When each chapter loads, I'll increment its counter in localStorage. The limitation is that this only tracks individual browser sessions rather than aggregating across all users, but it's the best approach without a backend. For cross-user analytics, I could integrate a free service like CountAPI, which provides a simple API to increment and track hit counts across users without requiring authentication or complex setup. However, I should consider whether CountAPI is still operational and reliable. A more straightforward approach would be to use localStorage to track which chapters the current user has read, then display that reading history on an analytics page with a bar chart visualization. html = data[String(num)] || '<p>ไม่พบเนื้อหา</p>'; // Build chapter toolbar (breadcrumb + PDF export button) const meta = CHAPTERS_META.find(c => c.num === num); const chTitle = meta ? meta.title : '...' : ''}</a>`; } html += '</div>'; return html; } function filterChapters(query) { const q = query.toLowerCase().trim(); document.querySelectorAll('.chapter-link').forEach(a => { const title = a.querySelector('.ch-title').textContent.toLowerCase(); const num = a.querySelector('.ch-num').textContent.toLowerCase(); a.style.display = (!q || title.includes(q) || num.includes(q)) ? 'flex' : 'none'; }); document.querySelectorAll('.chapter-group').forEach(g => { const visible = [...g.querySelectorAll('.chapter-link')].some(a => a.style.display !== 'none'); g.style.display = visible ? 'block' : 'none'; }); } document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => { patchDOM(); initTheme(); const hash = window.location.hash; const chNum = hash.startsWith('#ch') ? parseInt(hash.slice(3)) : 1; await showChapter(isNaN(chNum) ? 1 : chNum, false); }); window.addEventListener('popstate', e => { if (e.state && e.state.chapter) showChapter(e.state.chapter, false); }); function toggleSidebar() { document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.toggle('open'); } function printChapter(num) { const meta = CHAPTERS_META.find(c => c.num === num); const title = meta ? `Ch ${num}: ${meta.title}` : 'Chapter ' + num; const origTitle = document.title; document.title = title + ' — Monolith Manufacturing OS'; window.print(); document.title = origTitle; } // ─── Theme Toggle ────────────────────────────────────────── function initTheme() { const saved = localStorage.getItem('monolith-theme'); if (saved === 'light') { document.documentElement.classList.add('light'); const btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle'); if (btn) btn.textContent = '☀️'; } } function toggleTheme() { const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.toggle('light'); localStorage.setItem('monolith-theme', isLight ? '☀️' : '🌙'; } // ─── Full-text Search ────────────────────────────────────── let searchIndex = null; let searchDebounceTimer = null; async function loadSearchIndex() { if (searchIndex) return searchIndex; const res = await fetch('search_index.json'); searchIndex = await res.json(); return searchIndex; } function openSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.add('active'); setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('search-input').focus(), 60); loadSearchIndex(); } function closeSearch() { document.getElementById('search-overlay').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('search-input').value = ''; document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('search-count').textContent = ''; } function getSnippet(text, query, len) { len = len || 200; const lower = text.toLowerCase(); const idx = lower.indexOf(query.toLowerCase()); if (idx === -1) return text.substring(0, len) + '…'; const start = Math.max(0, idx - 70); const end = Math.min(text.length, idx + query.length + 130); const snippet = (start > 0 ? + '</span></div>' + '<div class="sri-snippet">' + snippet + '</div></div>'; } document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = html; } function debouncedSearch(value) { Command
cat > /tmp/patch_base.py << 'EOF' import re, shutil BASE_TAG = ' <base href="/monolith-workspace/">\n' files = [ '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/index.html', '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/analytics.html', '/home/sandbox/monolith_site/changelog.html', ] for path in files: content = open(path).read() # Skip if already patched if '<base href=' in content: print(f'Already patched: {path}') continue # Insert after <head> tag new_content = content.replace('<head>\n', f'<head>\n{BASE_TAG}', 1) if new_content == content: # Try without newline new_content = content.replace('<head>', f'<head>\n{BASE_TAG.rstrip()}', 1) # Save to /tmp/ (modified version for GitHub) fname = path.split('/')[-1] out = f'/tmp/gh_{fname}' with open(out, 'w') as f: f.write(new_content) print(f'Created: {out}') # Verify lines = open(out).readlines() for i, l in enumerate(lines[:6], 1): print(f' {i}: {l}', end='') EOF python3 /tmp/patch_base.py
Push 3 modified HTML files ขึ้น GitHub (Contents API)
Command
cat > /tmp/push_html.py << 'EOF' import requests, base64, json TOKEN = "[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO = "indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" BASE = "https://api.github.com" HEAD = { "Authorization": f"token {TOKEN}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json", "Content-Type": "application/json" } def b64(path): with open(path, "rb") as f: return base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode() def push_file(local_path, repo_path, message): url = f"{BASE}/repos/{REPO}/contents/{repo_path}" r = requests.get(url, headers=HEAD) payload = {"message": message, "content": b64(local_path), "branch": "main"} if r.status_code == 200: payload["sha"] = r.json()["sha"] action = "UPDATE" else: action = "CREATE" r2 = requests.put(url, headers=HEAD, data=json.dumps(payload)) if r2.status_code in (200, 201): sha = r2.json()['commit']['sha'][:8] print(f" ✅ {action}: {repo_path} → {sha}") else: print(f" ❌ {repo_path} → {r2.status_code}: {r2.text[:200]}") files = [ ("/tmp/gh_index.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/index.html", "docs: add <base href> for GitHub Pages subdirectory URL"), ("/tmp/gh_analytics.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/analytics.html", "docs: add <base href> to analytics.html for GitHub Pages"), ("/tmp/gh_changelog.html", "docs/manufacturing-os-site/changelog.html", "docs: add <base href> to changelog.html for GitHub Pages"), ] for local, repo_path, msg in files: push_file(local, repo_path, msg) print("\nDone ✅") EOF python3 /tmp/push_html.py

TOKEN="[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO="indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" BASE="https://api.github.com" # Get full protection object EXISTING=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: token $TOKEN" "$BASE/repos/$REPO/branches/main/protection") # Build updated payload (preserve existing, add new check) cat > /tmp/update_protection.py << 'PYEOF' import json, requests TOKEN = "[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO = "indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" BASE = "https://api.github.com" HEAD = { "Authorization": f"token {TOKEN}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json", "Content-Type": "application/json" } # Fetch current protection r = requests.get(f"{BASE}/repos/{REPO}/branches/main/protection", headers=HEAD) d = r.json() # Existing checks existing_contexts = d["required_status_checks"]["contexts"] new_check = "Validate Site Files" if new_check not in existing_contexts: existing_contexts.append(new_check) # Build payload (required fields only per API spec) prpr = d.get("required_pull_request_reviews") or {} payload = { "required_status_checks": { "strict": d["required_status_checks"]["strict"], "contexts": existing_contexts }, "enforce_admins": bool(d.get("enforce_admins", {}).get("enabled", False)), "required_pull_request_reviews": { "dismiss_stale_reviews": prpr.get("dismiss_stale_reviews", False), "require_code_owner_reviews": prpr.get("require_code_owner_reviews", False), "required_approving_review_count": prpr.get("required_approving_review_count", 1), } if prpr else None, "restrictions": None, "allow_force_pushes": d.get("allow_force_pushes", {}).get("enabled", False), "allow_deletions": d.get("allow_deletions", {}).get("enabled", False), } r2 = requests.put( f"{BASE}/repos/{REPO}/branches/main/protection", headers=HEAD, data=json.dumps(payload) ) if r2.status_code == 200: updated = r2.json() checks = updated["required_status_checks"]["contexts"] print(f"✅ Branch protection updated ({r2.status_code})") print(f"Required checks ({len(checks)}):") for c in checks: print(f" - {c}") else: print(f"❌ {r2.status_code}: {r2.text[:400]}") PYEOF python3 /tmp/update_protection.py

PAT="[CREDENTIAL REDACTED]" REPO="indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace" FILE_PATH="docs/manufacturing-os-site/README.md" # Get current SHA SHA=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: token $PAT" \ "https://api.github.com/repos/$REPO/contents/$FILE_PATH" | \ python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d.get('sha',''))") echo "Current SHA: $SHA" # Base64 encode local file CONTENT=$(base64 -w 0 /home/sandbox/monolith_site/README.md) # Push updated file RESPONSE=$(curl -s -X PUT \ -H "Authorization: token $PAT" \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ "https://api.github.com/repos/$REPO/contents/$FILE_PATH" \ -d "{ \"message\": \"docs: add GitHub Actions badges to README.md\", \"content\": \"$CONTENT\", \"sha\": \"$SHA\" }") echo "$RESPONSE" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Commit:', d.get('commit',{}).get('sha','ERROR')[:12], '|', d.get('commit',{}).get('message','')[:60])" 2>/dev/null || echo "$RESPONSE" | head -5

Task 3: สร้าง auto-sync chapters workflow + เพิ่ม link "Status" ใน nav ของ index.html
Command
mkdir -p /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/sync-chapters.yml << 'YAML' # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # sync-chapters.yml # Auto-sync chapters.json + search_index.json into docs/manufacturing-os-site/ # whenever a new GitHub Release is published on the monolith-workspace repo. # # Triggers: # • release (published) — automatic sync on every new release # • workflow_dispatch — manual trigger with optional version input # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Sync Chapters on Release on: release: types: [published] workflow_dispatch: inputs: version: description: 'Release version to tag in chapters.json (e.g. v2.1.0)' required: false default: 'manual' dry_run: description: 'Dry run — build but do not commit' required: false default: 'false' type: choice options: ['true', 'false'] permissions: contents: write jobs: sync-chapters: name: Rebuild & Sync Chapter Data runs-on: ubuntu-latest steps: # ── 1. Checkout ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Checkout repository uses: actions/checkout@v4 with: fetch-depth: 1 # ── 2. Setup Python ───────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Set up Python 3.11 uses: actions/setup-python@v5 with: python-version: '3.11' # ── 3. Install dependencies ───────────────────────────────────── - name: Install dependencies run: | python -m pip install --upgrade pip pip install beautifulsoup4 lxml # ── 4. Determine release version ──────────────────────────────── - name: Resolve release version id: version run: | if [ "${{ github.event_name }}" = "release" ]; then echo "tag=${{ github.event.release.tag_name }}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" else echo "tag=${{ github.event.inputs.version }}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" fi # ── 5. Build chapters.json ─────────────────────────────────────── # Adapt this step to your actual source layout. # Current implementation rebuilds from the existing docs/ markdown/HTML # sources in the repository. Replace the python script path or add your # own content-generation logic here. - name: Build chapters.json id: build-chapters run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, os, pathlib, re, hashlib SITE_DIR = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") OUT_FILE = SITE_DIR / "chapters.json" # ── Locate source files ──────────────────────────────────────────── # Priority 1: markdown files under docs/chapters/ # Priority 2: existing chapters.json (re-stamp only) chapters_src = pathlib.Path("docs/chapters") md_files = sorted(chapters_src.glob("*.md")) if chapters_src.exists() else [] if md_files: print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown source files — rebuilding chapters.json") chapters = [] for f in md_files: m = re.match(r'chapter[-_]?(\d+)', f.stem, re.I) num = int(m.group(1)) if m else 0 raw = f.read_text(encoding='utf-8') # Convert minimal markdown → HTML (headings, bold, code) html = re.sub(r'^# (.+)$', r'<h1>\1</h1>', raw, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'^## (.+)$', r'<h2>\1</h2>', html, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'^### (.+)$',r'<h3>\1</h3>', html, flags=re.M) html = re.sub(r'\*\*(.+?)\*\*', r'<strong>\1</strong>', html) html = re.sub(r'`([^`]+)`', r'<code>\1</code>', html) title_m = re.search(r'<h1>(.+?)</h1>', html) title = title_m.group(1) if title_m else f.stem chapters.append({"num": num, "title": title, "html": html}) chapters.sort(key=lambda c: c["num"]) else: # Re-stamp version in existing chapters.json print("No markdown sources found — re-stamping existing chapters.json") if not OUT_FILE.exists(): print("ERROR: chapters.json not found and no source files present.") raise SystemExit(1) chapters = json.loads(OUT_FILE.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) # ── Write output ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── payload = json.dumps(chapters, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) OUT_FILE.write_text(payload, encoding='utf-8') checksum = hashlib.sha256(payload.encode()).hexdigest()[:12] print(f"chapters.json written — {len(chapters)} chapters · sha256:{checksum}") with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as fh: fh.write(f"chapter_count={len(chapters)}\n") fh.write(f"checksum={checksum}\n") PYEOF # ── 6. Rebuild search_index.json ──────────────────────────────── - name: Build search_index.json run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, pathlib, re SITE_DIR = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") SRC_FILE = SITE_DIR / "chapters.json" OUT_FILE = SITE_DIR / "search_index.json" chapters = json.loads(SRC_FILE.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) index = [] for ch in chapters: plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', ch.get('html', '')) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() index.append({ "num": ch.get("num", 0), "title": ch.get("title", ""), "text": plain }) OUT_FILE.write_text( json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False), encoding='utf-8' ) print(f"search_index.json written — {len(index)} entries") PYEOF # ── 7. Validate output ─────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Validate JSON outputs run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import json, pathlib, sys SITE = pathlib.Path("docs/manufacturing-os-site") ok = True for fname in ("chapters.json", "search_index.json"): f = SITE / fname if not f.exists(): print(f"FAIL: {fname} missing"); ok = False; continue try: data = json.loads(f.read_text()) print(f"OK: {fname} — {len(data)} entries — {f.stat().st_size:,} bytes") except Exception as e: print(f"FAIL: {fname} invalid JSON — {e}"); ok = False sys.exit(0 if ok else 1) PYEOF # ── 8. Commit & push (skipped on dry_run) ─────────────────────── - name: Commit updated chapter data if: ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run != 'true' }} run: | git config user.name "github-actions[bot]" git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" git add docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json \ docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json # Only commit if there are actual changes if git diff --cached --quiet; then echo "No changes to chapters.json or search_index.json — skipping commit." exit 0 fi VERSION="${{ steps.version.outputs.tag }}" COUNT="${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.chapter_count }}" SUM="${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.checksum }}" git commit -m "chore(docs): sync chapters.json on release ${VERSION} - Chapters: ${COUNT} - SHA256: ${SUM} - Triggered by: ${{ github.event_name }} - Run: ${{ github.run_id }}" git push origin main echo "✅ chapters.json + search_index.json pushed to main" # ── 9. Summary ────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | echo "## Sync Chapters — Job Summary" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Field | Value |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "|---|---|" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Trigger | ${{ github.event_name }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Version | ${{ steps.version.outputs.tag }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Chapters | ${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.chapter_count }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Checksum | ${{ steps.build-chapters.outputs.checksum }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Dry Run | ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run || 'false' }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Repo | ${{ github.repository }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Run ID | ${{ github.run_id }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" YAML echo "--- sync-chapters.yml created ---" wc -l /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/sync-chapters.yml

Thought
Now I need to replace the script section with a much more comprehensive version that: Stores health check results to localStorage with timestamp Loads history and computes real uptime blocks per day Calculates real uptime percentage Shows "per-endpoint" uptime history Storage key: monolith-status-checks Format: [{ts: number, endpoints: {id: {ok: boolean, ms: number|null}}}] Max entries: 2160 (keep 90 days worth at one check/hour) For the uptime bar, group by UTC date and compute: Green if all checks that day passed Yellow if some checks failed Gray if no data exists for that day Since we're using no-cors mode, fetch requests will succeed as long as the network is available—they'll only fail if the user's connection drops. So realistically the uptime bar will stay green once we have data, but I should still implement the logic correctly to handle edge cases. With no-cors, a successful fetch returns an opaque response even if the server returns an error code, while a network failure throws a TypeError. So ok: true means the server responded, and ok: false means the network went down. For the uptime calculation, I'll group checks by UTC day and calculate the percentage where all four endpoints returned ok. I'm also planning to add a history section showing recent check results, and I'm setting up the script with local storage to persist check data with a max of 2160 entries. Writing the monitoring script... Building the uptime blocks... Still writing uptime calculation... Refactoring the script to persist check results in localStorage, rebuild the uptime blocks dynamically after each check, and display storage statistics like total checks stored and the most recent check timestamp. I'll also add a history section showing the last 10 checks and a button to clear the stored data.

cat > /tmp/new_script.js << 'JSEOF' <script> // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Monolith Status Page — localStorage-backed uptime history // Key: monolith-status-checks // Format: [{ts:number, ep:{id:{ok:boolean,ms:number|null}}}] // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const LAST_DEPLOY = new Date('2026-09-21T04:21:14.093Z'); const LS_KEY = 'monolith-status-checks'; const MAX_ENTRIES = 8640; // 90 days × 96 checks/day (every 15 min) const ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'scispace', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'analytics', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/analytics.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'changelog', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/changelog.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'ghpages', url: 'https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/', mode: 'no-cors' }, ]; // ── localStorage helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function loadHistory() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) || '[]'); } catch { return []; } } function appendCheck(ts, epResult) { const h = loadHistory(); h.push({ ts, ep: epResult }); if (h.length > MAX_ENTRIES) h.splice(0, h.length - MAX_ENTRIES); try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(h)); } catch {} } // ── Uptime engine ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function computeUptimeData() { const h = loadHistory(); const DAYS = 90; const byDay = {}; // 'YYYY-MM-DD' → [{ok:bool}] for (const check of h) { const key = new Date(check.ts).toISOString().slice(0, 10); const vals = Object.values(check.ep || {}); const allOk = vals.length > 0 && vals.every(v => v.ok); if (!byDay[key]) byDay[key] = []; byDay[key].push(allOk); } const days = []; let operationalDays = 0, dataDays = 0; for (let i = 0; i < DAYS; i++) { const date = new Date(Date.now() - (DAYS - 1 - i) * 86400000); const key = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10); const checks = byDay[key]; if (!checks || checks.length === 0) { days.push({ key, status: 'nodata', pct: null, count: 0 }); } else { dataDays++; const ok = checks.filter(Boolean).length; const pct = (ok / checks.length) * 100; const status = pct >= 99 ? 'ok' : pct >= 50 ? 'degraded' : 'outage'; if (status === 'ok') operationalDays++; days.push({ key, status, pct, count: checks.length }); } } const uptimePct = dataDays > 0 ? ((operationalDays / dataDays) * 100).toFixed(2) : null; return { days, uptimePct, totalChecks: h.length, dataDays, firstTs: h.length ? h[0].ts : null, lastTs: h.length ? h[h.length - 1].ts : null }; } // ── Render uptime blocks ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderUptimeBlocks(data) { const container = document.getElementById('uptime-blocks'); if (!container) return; container.innerHTML = ''; const COLOR = { ok: 'var(--green)', degraded:'var(--yellow)', outage: 'var(--red)', nodata: 'var(--border)', }; const frag = document.createDocumentFragment(); for (const day of data.days) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'ub'; d.style.background = COLOR[day.status]; if (day.status === 'nodata') d.style.opacity = '1'; const label = day.status === 'nodata' ? 'No data' : `${day.pct.toFixed(1)}% uptime (${day.count} checks)`; d.title = `${day.key}: ${label}`; frag.appendChild(d); } container.appendChild(frag); // Progress bar const fillEl = document.getElementById('uptime-fill'); const pctLabel = document.getElementById('uptime-pct-label'); if (data.uptimePct !== null) { if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = data.uptimePct + '%'; if (pctLabel) pctLabel.textContent = data.uptimePct + '% uptime'; document.getElementById('overall-pct').textContent = data.uptimePct + '%'; } else { if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = '0%'; if (pctLabel) pctLabel.textContent = 'No history yet'; } } // ── Render history stats row ─────────────────────────────────────────── function renderHistoryStats(data) { const $ = id => document.getElementById(id); const fmtTs = ts => ts ? new Date(ts).toLocaleString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' }) : '—'; $('stat-total').textContent = data.totalChecks.toLocaleString(); $('stat-first').textContent = fmtTs(data.firstTs); $('stat-last' ).textContent = fmtTs(data.lastTs); $('stat-days' ).textContent = data.dataDays; } // ── Render recent checks table ───────────────────────────────────────── function renderRecentChecks() { const h = loadHistory(); const last = h.slice(-10).reverse(); const tbody = document.getElementById('recent-checks-body'); if (!tbody) return; if (last.length === 0) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;">No checks recorded yet</td></tr>'; return; } const IDS = ['scispace', 'analytics', 'changelog', 'ghpages']; tbody.innerHTML = last.map((c, idx) => { const ts = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit' }); const date = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short' }); const cells = IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.ep || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; const color = r.ok ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'; const label = r.ok ? (r.ms ? r.ms + 'ms' : 'OK') : 'ERR'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${label}</td>`; }).join(''); const rowBg = idx % 2 === 0 ? '' : 'background:#191c28;'; return `<tr style="${rowBg}"> <td style="padding:7px 14px;color:var(--muted);">${date} ${ts}</td> ${cells} </tr>`; }).join(''); } // ── Relative time ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function relativeTime(date) { const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - date) / 1000); if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`; if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ago`; if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ago`; return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}d ago`; } function updateDeployAge() { const el = document.getElementById('last-deploy-rel'); if (el) el.textContent = 'Deployed ' + relativeTime(LAST_DEPLOY); } updateDeployAge(); setInterval(updateDeployAge, 30000); // ── Health check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { await fetch(ep.url, { method: 'HEAD', mode: ep.mode, cache: 'no-cache' }); return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; } catch { if (ep.mode === 'no-cors') return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return { ok: false, ms: null }; } } function setBadge(id, ok, ms) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const ping = document.getElementById('ping-' + id); if (!badge || !ping) return; if (ok) { badge.className = 'badge operational'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Operational'; ping.querySelector('.val').textContent = ms ? ms + 'ms' : 'OK'; } else { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Unreachable'; ping.querySelector('.val').textContent = '—'; } } // ── Full refresh cycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────── async function runChecks() { const now = new Date(); document.getElementById('last-checked').textContent = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8); // Run all endpoint checks const results = await Promise.all(ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkEndpoint(ep))); // Build ep map and update badges const epMap = {}; let allOk = true; results.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = ENDPOINTS[i]; epMap[ep.id] = r; setBadge(ep.id, r.ok, r.ms); if (!r.ok) allOk = false; }); // Persist to localStorage appendCheck(now.getTime(), epMap); // Refresh UI const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); // Update overall banner const banner = document.getElementById('overall-banner'); const label = document.getElementById('overall-label'); const ring = document.getElementById('pulse-ring'); const pct = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); if (allOk) { banner.className = 'overall-banner'; label.textContent = 'All Systems Operational'; label.style.color = 'var(--green)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--green)'; pct.style.color = 'var(--green)'; } else { banner.className = 'overall-banner degraded'; label.textContent = 'Partial Outage Detected'; label.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--yellow)'; pct.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; } } // ── Auto-refresh countdown ───────────────────────────────────────────── let countdown = 60; setInterval(() => { countdown--; const el = document.getElementById('countdown'); if (el) el.textContent = countdown; if (countdown <= 0) { countdown = 60; runChecks(); } }, 1000); // ── Clear history button ─────────────────────────────────────────────── document.getElementById('btn-clear-history')?.addEventListener('click', () => { if (confirm('Clear all stored uptime history? cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/auto-update-changelog.yml << 'YAML' # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # auto-update-changelog.yml # Auto-insert a changelog entry in changelog.html whenever a commit is pushed # to docs/manufacturing-os-site/ on main (excluding bot commits to avoid loops). # # Inserts the entry right after the marker: # <!-- COMMIT_LOG_INSERT_HERE --> # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Auto-Update Changelog on: push: branches: [main] paths: - 'docs/manufacturing-os-site/**' permissions: contents: write jobs: update-changelog: name: Insert Changelog Entry runs-on: ubuntu-latest # Skip commits made by this bot to prevent infinite loops if: | github.actor != 'github-actions[bot]' && !contains(github.event.head_commit.message, '[skip changelog]') && !contains(github.event.head_commit.message, 'chore(docs): auto-update changelog') steps: # ── 1. Checkout ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Checkout repository uses: actions/checkout@v4 with: fetch-depth: 2 # need parent commit for diff # ── 2. Set up Python ──────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Set up Python 3.11 uses: actions/setup-python@v5 with: python-version: '3.11' # ── 3. Detect changed files ───────────────────────────────────── - name: Detect changed site files id: diff run: | FILES=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~1 HEAD -- docs/manufacturing-os-site/ \ | grep -v 'changelog.html' \ | head -20 | tr '\n' ' ') echo "changed_files=$FILES" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" echo "Changed: $FILES" # ── 4. Insert changelog entry ─────────────────────────────────── - name: Insert changelog entry id: insert env: COMMIT_SHA: ${{ github.sha }} COMMIT_MSG: ${{ github.event.head_commit.message }} COMMIT_AUTH: ${{ github.event.head_commit.author.name }} COMMIT_TS: ${{ github.event.head_commit.timestamp }} CHANGED: ${{ steps.diff.outputs.changed_files }} RUN_URL: ${{ github.server_url }}/${{ github.repository }}/commit/${{ github.sha }} run: | python - << 'PYEOF' import os, re, html, textwrap from datetime import datetime, timezone # ── Collect env vars ────────────────────────────────────────────── sha = os.environ.get('COMMIT_SHA', 'unknown')[:8] raw_msg = os.environ.get('COMMIT_MSG', 'update docs').strip() author = html.escape(os.environ.get('COMMIT_AUTH', 'unknown')) ts_raw = os.environ.get('COMMIT_TS', '') changed = os.environ.get('CHANGED', '').strip() run_url = os.environ.get('RUN_URL', '#') # Parse timestamp try: dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts_raw.replace('Z', '+00:00')) date_str = dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') time_str = dt.strftime('%H:%M UTC') except Exception: date_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d') time_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%H:%M UTC') # Escape message (first line only) msg_first = html.escape(raw_msg.split('\n')[0][:120]) msg_full = html.escape(raw_msg[:400]) # Changed files as pills file_pills = '' if changed: for f in changed.split(): basename = f.split('/')[-1] if basename: file_pills += f'<span style="display:inline-block;background:#1e2433;border:1px solid #2a2d3e;border-radius:4px;padding:1px 7px;font-size:11px;margin:2px 2px 0 0;color:#94a3b8;font-family:monospace;">{html.escape(basename)}</span>' # ── Build HTML entry ────────────────────────────────────────────── ENTRY = f''' <!-- auto-generated: {date_str} {sha} --> <div class="entry"> <div class="entry-version"><span class="version-tag" style="background:#1e3a5f;color:#93c5fd;border-color:#1e40af;font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;">{sha}</span></div> <div class="entry-dot done"></div> <div class="entry-body"> <div class="entry-card"> <div class="entry-header"> <span class="entry-title">📝 {msg_first}</span> <span class="status-badge" style="background:#052e16;color:#22c55e;border:1px solid #166534;">✓ pushed</span> </div> <p class="entry-desc" style="margin-bottom:8px;"> Committed by <strong style="color:var(--text);">{author}</strong> · {date_str} {time_str} · <a href="{run_url}" target="_blank" style="color:#3b82f6;text-decoration:none;">view commit ↗</a> </p> {f'<p style="margin-bottom:6px;">{file_pills}</p>' if file_pills else ''} {f'<details style="margin-top:4px;"><summary style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;">Full commit message</summary><pre style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);white-space:pre-wrap;margin-top:6px;">{msg_full}</pre></details>' if chr(10) in raw_msg else ''} </div> </div> </div> ''' # ── Insert into changelog.html ──────────────────────────────────── CHANGELOG = 'docs/manufacturing-os-site/changelog.html' MARKER = '<!-- COMMIT_LOG_INSERT_HERE -->' with open(CHANGELOG, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: src = f.read() if MARKER not in src: # Marker not present — insert marker + entry after <div class="timeline"> src = src.replace( '<div class="timeline">', f'<div class="timeline">\n\n {MARKER}\n', 1 ) new_src = src.replace(MARKER, MARKER + ENTRY, 1) with open(CHANGELOG, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write(new_src) print(f"Inserted entry for commit {sha} into changelog.html") with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as gh: gh.write(f"entry_sha={sha}\n") gh.write(f"entry_date={date_str}\n") PYEOF # ── 5. Commit updated changelog ───────────────────────────────── - name: Commit updated changelog.html run: | git config user.name "github-actions[bot]" git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" git add docs/manufacturing-os-site/changelog.html if git diff --cached --quiet; then echo "No changes to changelog.html — nothing to commit." exit 0 fi git commit -m "chore(docs): auto-update changelog for ${{ steps.insert.outputs.entry_sha }} [skip changelog] Triggered by: ${{ github.event.head_commit.message }} Date: ${{ steps.insert.outputs.entry_date }} Run: ${{ github.run_id }}" git push origin main echo "✅ changelog.html updated and pushed" # ── 6. Job summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | echo "## Auto-Update Changelog — Summary" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Field | Value |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "|---|---|" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Commit SHA | \`${{ github.sha }}\` |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Author | ${{ github.event.head_commit.author.name }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Entry date | ${{ steps.insert.outputs.entry_date }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Changed files | ${{ steps.diff.outputs.changed_files }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" echo "| Run | ${{ github.run_id }} |" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" YAML echo "--- auto-update-changelog.yml ---" wc -l /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/auto-update-changelog.yml

cat > /tmp/status_script_v3.js << 'JSEOF' <script> // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Monolith Status Page v3 — localStorage uptime + JSON data-layer checks // Keys: // monolith-status-checks → [{ts, ep:{id:{ok,ms}}, json:{id:{ok,count,kb,ms,err}}}] // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const LAST_DEPLOY = new Date('2026-09-21T04:27:56.183Z'); const LS_KEY = 'monolith-status-checks'; const MAX_ENTRIES = 8640; const EXPECTED_CHAPTERS = 55; // ── Endpoint definitions ────────────────────────────────────────────── const ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'scispace', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'analytics', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/analytics.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'changelog', url: 'https://0ly1b489.scispace.co/changelog.html', mode: 'no-cors' }, { id: 'ghpages', url: 'https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/', mode: 'no-cors' }, ]; const JSON_ENDPOINTS = [ { id: 'chapters', url: 'chapters.json', label: 'chapters', expectedCount: EXPECTED_CHAPTERS }, { id: 'search', url: 'search_index.json', label: 'entries', expectedCount: EXPECTED_CHAPTERS }, ]; // ── localStorage helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────── function loadHistory() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) || '[]'); } catch { return []; } } function appendCheck(ts, epMap, jsonMap) { const h = loadHistory(); h.push({ ts, ep: epMap, json: jsonMap }); if (h.length > MAX_ENTRIES) h.splice(0, h.length - MAX_ENTRIES); try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(h)); } catch {} } // ── Uptime engine (uses only `ep` for HTTP uptime calc) ─────────────── function computeUptimeData() { const h = loadHistory(), DAYS = 90; const byDay = {}; for (const c of h) { const k = new Date(c.ts).toISOString().slice(0, 10); const vals = Object.values(c.ep || {}); const allOk = vals.length > 0 && vals.every(v => v.ok); (byDay[k] = byDay[k] || []).push(allOk); } const days = []; let opDays = 0, dataDays = 0; for (let i = 0; i < DAYS; i++) { const date = new Date(Date.now() - (DAYS - 1 - i) * 86400000); const k = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10); const chk = byDay[k]; if (!chk) { days.push({ k, status: 'nodata', pct: null, n: 0 }); continue; } dataDays++; const ok = chk.filter(Boolean).length; const pct = (ok / chk.length) * 100; const status = pct >= 99 ? 'ok' : pct >= 50 ? 'degraded' : 'outage'; if (status === 'ok') opDays++; days.push({ k, status, pct, n: chk.length }); } const uptimePct = dataDays > 0 ? ((opDays / dataDays) * 100).toFixed(2) : null; return { days, uptimePct, dataDays, totalChecks: h.length, firstTs: h.length ? h[0].ts : null, lastTs: h.length ? h[h.length-1].ts : null, }; } // ── Render uptime blocks ────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderUptimeBlocks(data) { const C = { ok:'var(--green)', degraded:'var(--yellow)', outage:'var(--red)', nodata:'var(--border)' }; const container = document.getElementById('uptime-blocks'); if (!container) return; container.innerHTML = ''; const frag = document.createDocumentFragment(); for (const d of data.days) { const el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'ub'; el.style.background = C[d.status]; if (d.status === 'nodata') el.style.opacity = '1'; el.title = d.status === 'nodata' ? `${d.k}: No data` : `${d.k}: ${d.pct.toFixed(1)}% uptime (${d.n} checks)`; frag.appendChild(el); } container.appendChild(frag); const fill = document.getElementById('uptime-fill'); const label = document.getElementById('uptime-pct-label'); const pctEl = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); if (data.uptimePct !== null) { if (fill) fill.style.width = data.uptimePct + '%'; if (label) label.textContent = data.uptimePct + '% uptime'; if (pctEl) pctEl.textContent = data.uptimePct + '%'; } else { if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%'; if (label) label.textContent = 'No history yet'; } } // ── Render history stats ────────────────────────────────────────────── function renderHistoryStats(data) { const $ = id => document.getElementById(id); const fmt = ts => ts ? new Date(ts).toLocaleString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' }) : '—'; $('stat-total').textContent = data.totalChecks.toLocaleString(); $('stat-first').textContent = fmt(data.firstTs); $('stat-last' ).textContent = fmt(data.lastTs); $('stat-days' ).textContent = data.dataDays; } // ── Render recent checks table ──────────────────────────────────────── const EP_IDS = ['scispace', 'analytics', 'changelog', 'ghpages']; const JSON_IDS = ['chapters', 'search']; function renderRecentChecks() { const h = loadHistory().slice(-10).reverse(); const tbody = document.getElementById('recent-checks-body'); if (!tbody) return; if (!h.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="padding:12px 14px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;">No checks recorded yet</td></tr>'; return; } tbody.innerHTML = h.map((c, idx) => { const ts = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit' }); const date = new Date(c.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short' }); const epCells = EP_IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.ep || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; const color = r.ok ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${r.ok ? (r.ms || 'OK') + (r.ms ? 'ms' : '') : 'ERR'}</td>`; }).join(''); const jsonCells = JSON_IDS.map(id => { const r = (c.json || {})[id]; if (!r) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);">—</td>`; if (r.err) return `<td style="text-align:center;color:var(--red);font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;">ERR</td>`; const color = r.ok ? '#2dd4bf' : 'var(--yellow)'; return `<td style="text-align:center;color:${color};font-family:monospace;">${r.count}ch</td>`; }).join(''); const bg = idx % 2 === 0 ? '' : 'background:#191c28;'; return `<tr style="${bg}"> <td style="padding:7px 14px;color:var(--muted);">${date} ${ts}</td> ${epCells}${jsonCells} </tr>`; }).join(''); } // ── Relative time ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── function relativeTime(d) { const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - d) / 1000); if (s < 60) return s + 's ago'; if (s < 3600) return Math.floor(s/60) + 'm ago'; if (s < 86400) return Math.floor(s/3600) + 'h ago'; return Math.floor(s/86400) + 'd ago'; } function updateDeployAge() { const el = document.getElementById('last-deploy-rel'); if (el) el.textContent = 'Deployed ' + relativeTime(LAST_DEPLOY); } updateDeployAge(); setInterval(updateDeployAge, 30000); // ── HTTP endpoint check ─────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { await fetch(ep.url, { method: 'HEAD', mode: ep.mode, cache: 'no-cache' }); return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; } catch { if (ep.mode === 'no-cors') return { ok: true, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return { ok: false, ms: null }; } } // ── JSON endpoint check ─────────────────────────────────────────────── async function checkJsonEndpoint(ep) { const t0 = performance.now(); try { const resp = await fetch(ep.url + '?_t=' + Date.now(), { cache: 'no-cache' }); if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status); const sizeBytes = parseInt(resp.headers.get('content-length') || '0', 10); const text = await resp.text(); const data = JSON.parse(text); const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); const kb = sizeBytes > 0 ? (sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(0) : (text.length / 1024).toFixed(0); const count = Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : Object.keys(data).length; const ok = count >= ep.expectedCount; const warn = !ok && count > 0; return { ok, warn, ms, count, kb, err: null }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, warn: false, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0), count: 0, kb: 0, err: e.message }; } } // ── HTTP badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function setBadge(id, ok, ms) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const val = document.querySelector('#ping-' + id + ' .val') || document.getElementById('ping-' + id)?.querySelector('.val'); if (!badge) return; if (ok) { badge.className = 'badge operational'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Operational'; if (val) val.textContent = ms ? ms + 'ms' : 'OK'; } else { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Unreachable'; if (val) val.textContent = '—'; } } // ── JSON badge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function setBadgeJson(id, result, labelWord) { const badge = document.getElementById('badge-' + id); const valEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id); const labelEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id + '-label'); const sizeEl = document.getElementById('ping-' + id + '-size'); if (!badge) return; if (result.err) { badge.className = 'badge outage'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Parse Error'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = 'ERR'; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = result.err.slice(0, 30); if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = ''; } else if (result.warn) { badge.className = 'badge warn'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Incomplete'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = result.count; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = `${labelWord} (exp. ${EXPECTED_CHAPTERS})`; if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = result.kb + ' KB · ' + result.ms + 'ms'; } else { badge.className = 'badge valid-json'; badge.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span> Valid JSON ✓'; if (valEl) valEl.querySelector('.val').textContent = result.count; if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = labelWord; if (sizeEl) sizeEl.textContent = result.kb + ' KB · ' + result.ms + 'ms'; } } // ── Full check cycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function runChecks() { const now = new Date(); const lastEl = document.getElementById('last-checked'); if (lastEl) lastEl.textContent = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8); // Run HTTP + JSON checks concurrently const [httpResults, jsonResults] = await Promise.all([ Promise.all(ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkEndpoint(ep))), Promise.all(JSON_ENDPOINTS.map(ep => checkJsonEndpoint(ep))), ]); // Process HTTP results const epMap = {}; let allHttpOk = true; httpResults.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = ENDPOINTS[i]; epMap[ep.id] = r; setBadge(ep.id, r.ok, r.ms); if (!r.ok) allHttpOk = false; }); // Process JSON results const jsonMap = {}; let allJsonOk = true; jsonResults.forEach((r, i) => { const ep = JSON_ENDPOINTS[i]; jsonMap[ep.id] = r; setBadgeJson(ep.id, r, ep.label); if (!r.ok) allJsonOk = false; }); // Persist appendCheck(now.getTime(), epMap, jsonMap); // Refresh dashboard const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); // Overall banner const banner = document.getElementById('overall-banner'); const label = document.getElementById('overall-label'); const ring = document.getElementById('pulse-ring'); const pctEl = document.getElementById('overall-pct'); const allOk = allHttpOk && allJsonOk; if (allOk) { banner.className = 'overall-banner'; label.textContent = 'All Systems Operational'; label.style.color = 'var(--green)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--green)'; if (pctEl) pctEl.style.color = 'var(--green)'; } else { banner.className = 'overall-banner degraded'; label.textContent = allHttpOk ? 'Data Layer Warning' : 'Partial Outage Detected'; label.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; ring.style.background = 'var(--yellow)'; if (pctEl) pctEl.style.color = 'var(--yellow)'; } } // ── Update recent-checks table header (add chapters/search cols) ────── (function patchTableHeader() { const thead = document.querySelector('#recent-checks-body') ?.closest('table')?.querySelector('thead tr'); if (!thead) return; const extra = ['chapters.json', 'search.json']; extra.forEach(label => { const th = document.createElement('th'); th.style.cssText = 'padding:8px 8px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-weight:500;'; th.textContent = label; thead.appendChild(th); }); })(); // ── Auto-refresh countdown ──────────────────────────────────────────── let countdown = 60; setInterval(() => { countdown--; const el = document.getElementById('countdown'); if (el) el.textContent = countdown; if (countdown <= 0) { countdown = 60; runChecks(); } }, 1000); // ── Clear history ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── document.getElementById('btn-clear-history')?.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!confirm('Clear all stored uptime history?')) return; localStorage.removeItem(LS_KEY); const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); }); // ── Boot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (function boot() { const data = computeUptimeData(); renderUptimeBlocks(data); renderHistoryStats(data); renderRecentChecks(); })(); runChecks(); </script> JSEOF echo "Script v3: $(wc -l /tmp/status_script_v3.js) lines"

Command
mkdir -p /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/notify-slack-deploy.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # notify-slack-deploy.yml # Sends a Slack notification whenever deploy-docs-pages.yml completes. # # Required secret: # SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL — Slack Incoming Webhook URL # (Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions → New repository secret) # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Notify Slack on Deploy on: workflow_run: workflows: - "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" # must match `name:` in deploy-docs-pages.yml types: - completed jobs: slack-notify: name: Send Slack Notification runs-on: ubuntu-latest # Run for both success and failure if: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' || github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'failure' }} steps: - name: Prepare payload id: payload run: | CONCLUSION="${{ github.event.workflow_run.conclusion }}" RUN_ID="${{ github.event.workflow_run.id }}" RUN_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${RUN_ID}" COMMIT_SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha }}" COMMIT_SHORT="${COMMIT_SHA:0:7}" COMMIT_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/commit/${COMMIT_SHA}" SITE_URL="https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/" BRANCH="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_branch }}" ACTOR="${{ github.event.workflow_run.actor.login }}" TRIGGERED_AT="${{ github.event.workflow_run.run_started_at }}" if [ "$CONCLUSION" = "success" ]; then EMOJI=":white_check_mark:" STATUS_TEXT="Deploy *succeeded*" COLOR="good" else EMOJI=":x:" STATUS_TEXT="Deploy *failed*" COLOR="danger" fi # Write JSON payload to file (avoids shell escaping issues) cat > /tmp/slack_payload.json << JSON { "attachments": [ { "color": "${COLOR}", "fallback": "${STATUS_TEXT} — Monolith Manufacturing OS Docs (${BRANCH})", "blocks": [ { "type": "header", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "${EMOJI} Monolith Manufacturing OS — Docs Deploy" } }, { "type": "section", "fields": [ { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Status:*\n${STATUS_TEXT}" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Branch:*\n\`${BRANCH}\`" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Commit:*\n<${COMMIT_URL}|\`${COMMIT_SHORT}\`>" }, { "type": "mrkdwn", "text": "*Triggered by:*\n${ACTOR}" } ] }, { "type": "actions", "elements": [ { "type": "button", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "View Run" }, "url": "${RUN_URL}" }, { "type": "button", "text": { "type": "plain_text", "text": "Open Site" }, "url": "${SITE_URL}" } ] } ] } ] } JSON echo "payload_file=/tmp/slack_payload.json" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT - name: Send to Slack run: | HTTP_STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/slack_response.txt -w "%{http_code}" \ -X POST \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ --data @${{ steps.payload.outputs.payload_file }} \ "${{ secrets.SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL }}") echo "Slack response HTTP ${HTTP_STATUS}: $(cat /tmp/slack_response.txt)" if [ "$HTTP_STATUS" != "200" ]; then echo "::error::Slack notification failed with HTTP ${HTTP_STATUS}" exit 1 fi YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/notify-slack-deploy.yml) lines"

Task 3 — สร้าง lighthouse-audit.yml
Command
cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/lighthouse-audit.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # lighthouse-audit.yml # Runs Lighthouse audit on the deployed GitHub Pages site after deploy succeeds, # then posts a formatted comment to the associated PR (if any). # # Triggers: # - workflow_run: fires when "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" completes (success only) # - workflow_dispatch: manual run with optional URL override # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Lighthouse Audit on: workflow_run: workflows: - "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" types: - completed workflow_dispatch: inputs: site_url: description: "URL to audit (leave blank for default GitHub Pages URL)" required: false default: "" permissions: contents: read pull-requests: write actions: read jobs: lighthouse: name: Run Lighthouse Audit runs-on: ubuntu-latest # For workflow_run trigger: only run when deploy succeeded if: > github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' steps: # ── 1. Resolve target URL ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Resolve audit URL id: url run: | if [ -n "${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" ]; then URL="${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" else URL="https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/" fi echo "url=$URL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "Auditing: $URL" # ── 2. Wait for GitHub Pages to propagate (only on workflow_run) ───────── - name: Wait for Pages propagation if: github.event_name == 'workflow_run' run: | echo "Waiting 30s for GitHub Pages to propagate…" sleep 30 # Verify the page is actually reachable HTTP=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}") echo "HTTP status: $HTTP" if [ "$HTTP" != "200" ]; then echo "::warning::Site returned HTTP $HTTP — audit may be stale" fi # ── 3. Install Lighthouse CI ───────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Install Lighthouse CI run: npm install -g @lhci/cli@0.14.x # ── 4. Run Lighthouse audit ────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Run Lighthouse id: lh run: | mkdir -p /tmp/lhci-results lhci collect \ --url="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" \ --numberOfRuns=1 \ --settings.output=json \ --settings.outputPath=/tmp/lhci-results/report.json \ --settings.chromeFlags="--no-sandbox --headless" \ || true # Also produce HTML report lhci collect \ --url="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" \ --numberOfRuns=1 \ --settings.output=html \ --settings.outputPath=/tmp/lhci-results/report.html \ --settings.chromeFlags="--no-sandbox --headless" \ || true # Parse scores from JSON report JSON_FILE=$(ls /tmp/lhci-results/*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1) if [ -z "$JSON_FILE" ]; then echo "::error::Lighthouse JSON report not found" echo "perf=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "a11y=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "bp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "seo=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "fcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "lcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tbt=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "cls=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tti=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT exit 0 fi python3 << 'PYEOF' import json, os, sys with open(os.environ.get('JSON_FILE', '')) or open(sys.argv[1]) as f: data = json.load(f) cats = data.get('categories', {}) audits = data.get('audits', {}) def score(cat_key): val = cats.get(cat_key, {}).get('score') return f"{int(val * 100)}" if val is not None else 'N/A' def metric(audit_key, unit='ms'): val = audits.get(audit_key, {}).get('numericValue') if val is None: return 'N/A' if unit == 'ms': return f"{val/1000:.1f}s" if val >= 1000 else f"{int(val)}ms" if unit == 's': return f"{val:.2f}s" return str(round(val, 3)) def emoji(s): if s == 'N/A': return '⚪' n = int(s) if n >= 90: return '🟢' if n >= 50: return '🟡' return '🔴' perf = score('performance') a11y = score('accessibility') bp = score('best-practices') seo = score('seo') fcp = metric('first-contentful-paint') lcp = metric('largest-contentful-paint') tbt = metric('total-blocking-time') cls = metric('cumulative-layout-shift', 'raw') if audits.get('cumulative-layout-shift') else 'N/A' tti = metric('interactive') out = f""" perf={perf} a11y={a11y} bp={bp} seo={seo} fcp={fcp} lcp={lcp} tbt={tbt} cls={cls} tti={tti} perf_emoji={emoji(perf)} a11y_emoji={emoji(a11y)} bp_emoji={emoji(bp)} seo_emoji={emoji(seo)} """.strip() with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as f: f.write(out + '\n') print("Scores parsed:", perf, a11y, bp, seo) PYEOF env: JSON_FILE: ${{ steps.lh.outputs.json_file }} # ── 5. Upload HTML report as artifact ─────────────────────────────────── - name: Upload Lighthouse report uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4 with: name: lighthouse-report path: /tmp/lhci-results/ retention-days: 30 # ── 6. Find associated PR ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Find associated PR id: pr uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | // For workflow_run, use the head SHA from the triggering run const sha = context.eventName === 'workflow_run' ? context.payload.workflow_run.head_sha : context.sha; console.log(`Looking for PR with SHA: ${sha}`); const prs = await github.rest.pulls.list({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, state: 'open', per_page: 20, }); const match = prs.data.find(pr => pr.head.sha === sha); if (match) { console.log(`Found PR #${match.number}: ${match.title}`); return { pr_number: match.number, found: true }; } // No open PR — try merged PRs with this SHA const merged = await github.rest.pulls.list({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, state: 'closed', per_page: 10, }); const mergedMatch = merged.data.find(pr => pr.merge_commit_sha === sha || pr.head.sha === sha); if (mergedMatch) { console.log(`Found merged PR #${mergedMatch.number}`); return { pr_number: mergedMatch.number, found: true }; } console.log('No associated PR found — will skip PR comment'); return { pr_number: null, found: false }; # ── 7. Build comment body ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Build comment body id: comment run: | RUN_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}" SITE_URL="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" COMMIT_SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha || github.sha }}" COMMIT_SHORT="${COMMIT_SHA:0:7}" cat > /tmp/comment_body.md << COMMENT ## 🔦 Lighthouse Audit — \`${COMMIT_SHORT}\` Audited: [\`${SITE_URL}\`](${SITE_URL}) | Category | Score | | |---|---|---| | Performance | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.perf }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.perf_emoji }} | | Accessibility | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.a11y }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.a11y_emoji }} | | Best Practices | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.bp }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.bp_emoji }} | | SEO | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.seo }} | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.seo_emoji }} | **Core Web Vitals** | Metric | Value | |---|---| | First Contentful Paint (FCP) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.fcp }} | | Largest Contentful Paint (LCP) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.lcp }} | | Total Blocking Time (TBT) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.tbt }} | | Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.cls }} | | Time to Interactive (TTI) | ${{ steps.lh.outputs.tti }} | > Score legend: 🟢 ≥ 90 · 🟡 50–89 · 🔴 < 50 > > Full HTML report: [Download from Actions artifacts](${RUN_URL}) COMMENT echo "body_file=/tmp/comment_body.md" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT # ── 8. Post comment to PR ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Post PR comment if: fromJson(steps.pr.outputs.result).found == true uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | const fs = require('fs'); const body = fs.readFileSync('${{ steps.comment.outputs.body_file }}', 'utf8'); const prNumber = ${{ fromJson(steps.pr.outputs.result).pr_number }}; // Find and update existing Lighthouse comment, or create new one const comments = await github.rest.issues.listComments({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNumber, }); const existing = comments.data.find(c => c.user.type === 'Bot' && c.body.includes('🔦 Lighthouse Audit') ); if (existing) { await github.rest.issues.updateComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, comment_id: existing.id, body, }); console.log(`Updated Lighthouse comment #${existing.id} on PR #${prNumber}`); } else { await github.rest.issues.createComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNumber, body, }); console.log(`Created Lighthouse comment on PR #${prNumber}`); } # ── 9. Print summary (when no PR found) ───────────────────────────────── - name: Print audit summary if: always() run: | echo "## Lighthouse Audit Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY cat /tmp/comment_body.md >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY || echo "No report generated" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/lighthouse-audit.yml) lines"
