cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/lighthouse-audit.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # lighthouse-audit.yml # Runs Lighthouse audit on the deployed GitHub Pages site after deploy succeeds, # then posts a formatted comment to the associated PR (if any). # # Triggers: # - workflow_run: fires when "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" completes (success only) # - workflow_dispatch: manual run with optional URL override # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Lighthouse Audit on: workflow_run: workflows: - "Deploy Manufacturing OS Docs" types: - completed workflow_dispatch: inputs: site_url: description: "URL to audit (leave blank for GitHub Pages default)" required: false default: "" permissions: contents: read pull-requests: write actions: read env: DEFAULT_URL: "https://indetailsgroup-hue.github.io/monolith-workspace/" jobs: lighthouse: name: Run Lighthouse Audit runs-on: ubuntu-latest if: > github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' steps: # ── 1. Resolve target URL ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Resolve audit URL id: url run: | if [ -n "${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" ]; then URL="${{ github.event.inputs.site_url }}" else URL="${DEFAULT_URL}" fi echo "url=${URL}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "Auditing: ${URL}" # ── 2. Wait for GitHub Pages propagation ──────────────────────────────── - name: Wait for Pages propagation if: github.event_name == 'workflow_run' run: | echo "Waiting 30s for GitHub Pages…" sleep 30 HTTP=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}") echo "HTTP status: ${HTTP}" if [ "$HTTP" != "200" ]; then echo "::warning::Site returned HTTP ${HTTP} — audit may target stale content" fi # ── 3. Install Lighthouse CLI ──────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Install Lighthouse run: | npm install -g lighthouse@12 lighthouse --version # ── 4. Run Lighthouse ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Run Lighthouse audit id: audit run: | mkdir -p /tmp/lh lighthouse "${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" \ --output=json \ --output=html \ --output-path=/tmp/lh/report \ --chrome-flags="--no-sandbox --headless --disable-gpu" \ --quiet \ || true # lighthouse writes /tmp/lh/report.report.json and /tmp/lh/report.report.html JSON_FILE="/tmp/lh/report.report.json" HTML_FILE="/tmp/lh/report.report.html" if [ ! -f "$JSON_FILE" ]; then echo "::error::Lighthouse report not generated" echo "perf=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "a11y=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "bp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "seo=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "fcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "lcp=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tbt=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "cls=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "tti=N/A" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "perf_emoji=⚪" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "a11y_emoji=⚪" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "bp_emoji=⚪" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT echo "seo_emoji=⚪" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT exit 0 fi # Parse scores with Python python3 - "$JSON_FILE" "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" << 'PYEOF' import json, sys json_file = sys.argv[1] output_file = sys.argv[2] with open(json_file) as f: data = json.load(f) cats = data.get('categories', {}) audits = data.get('audits', {}) def score(key): v = cats.get(key, {}).get('score') return str(int(v * 100)) if v is not None else 'N/A' def metric(key): v = audits.get(key, {}).get('numericValue') if v is None: return 'N/A' if key == 'cumulative-layout-shift': return f"{v:.3f}" return f"{v/1000:.1f}s" if v >= 1000 else f"{int(v)}ms" def emoji(s): if s == 'N/A': return '⚪' n = int(s) return '🟢' if n >= 90 else ('🟡' if n >= 50 else '🔴') scores = { 'perf': score('performance'), 'a11y': score('accessibility'), 'bp': score('best-practices'), 'seo': score('seo'), } metrics = { 'fcp': metric('first-contentful-paint'), 'lcp': metric('largest-contentful-paint'), 'tbt': metric('total-blocking-time'), 'cls': metric('cumulative-layout-shift'), 'tti': metric('interactive'), } with open(output_file, 'a') as out: for k, v in scores.items(): out.write(f"{k}={v}\n") out.write(f"{k}_emoji={emoji(v)}\n") for k, v in metrics.items(): out.write(f"{k}={v}\n") print("Scores:", scores) PYEOF # ── 5. Upload HTML report as artifact ──────────────────────────────────── - name: Upload Lighthouse report if: always() uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4 with: name: lighthouse-report-${{ github.run_id }} path: /tmp/lh/ retention-days: 30 # ── 6. Find associated PR ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Find associated PR id: pr uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | const sha = context.eventName === 'workflow_run' ? context.payload.workflow_run.head_sha : context.sha; console.log(`Searching for PR with SHA: ${sha}`); for (const state of ['open', 'closed']) { const { data } = await github.rest.pulls.list({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, state, per_page: 30, }); const match = data.find(pr => pr.head.sha === sha || pr.merge_commit_sha === sha ); if (match) { console.log(`Found PR #${match.number}: ${match.title}`); return { pr_number: match.number, found: true }; } } console.log('No associated PR — will skip PR comment'); return { pr_number: null, found: false }; # ── 7. Compose comment body ─────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Compose comment body id: comment run: | RUN_URL="https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}" SITE_URL="${{ steps.url.outputs.url }}" SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha || github.sha }}" SHORT="${SHA:0:7}" # Use printf to avoid heredoc quoting issues with emoji printf '%s\n' \ "## 🔦 Lighthouse Audit — \`${SHORT}\`" \ "" \ "Audited: [\`${SITE_URL}\`](${SITE_URL})" \ "" \ "| Category | Score | |" \ "|---|:---:|---|" \ "| Performance | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.perf }} | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.perf_emoji }} |" \ "| Accessibility | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.a11y }} | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.a11y_emoji }} |" \ "| Best Practices | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.bp }} | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.bp_emoji }} |" \ "| SEO | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.seo }} | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.seo_emoji }} |" \ "" \ "**Core Web Vitals**" \ "" \ "| Metric | Value |" \ "|---|---|" \ "| First Contentful Paint (FCP) | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.fcp }} |" \ "| Largest Contentful Paint (LCP) | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.lcp }} |" \ "| Total Blocking Time (TBT) | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.tbt }} |" \ "| Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS) | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.cls }} |" \ "| Time to Interactive (TTI) | ${{ steps.audit.outputs.tti }} |" \ "" \ "> 🟢 ≥ 90 · 🟡 50–89 · 🔴 < 50" \ ">" \ "> Full HTML report: [Download artifact](${RUN_URL})" \ > /tmp/lh_comment.md echo "body_file=/tmp/lh_comment.md" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT # ── 8. Post / update comment on PR ─────────────────────────────────────── - name: Post comment to PR if: fromJson(steps.pr.outputs.result).found == true uses: actions/github-script@v7 with: script: | const fs = require('fs'); const body = fs.readFileSync('${{ steps.comment.outputs.body_file }}', 'utf8'); const prNum = fromJson('${{ steps.pr.outputs.result }}').pr_number; const { data: comments } = await github.rest.issues.listComments({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNum, }); const existing = comments.find(c => c.user.type === 'Bot' && c.body.includes('🔦 Lighthouse Audit') ); if (existing) { await github.rest.issues.updateComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, comment_id: existing.id, body, }); console.log(`Updated comment #${existing.id} on PR #${prNum}`); } else { await github.rest.issues.createComment({ owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, issue_number: prNum, body, }); console.log(`Created comment on PR #${prNum}`); } # ── 9. Write job summary (always) ───────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | if [ -f /tmp/lh_comment.md ]; then cat /tmp/lh_comment.md >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY else echo "Lighthouse audit did not produce a report." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY fi YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/lighthouse-audit.yml) lines"

The user wants to: Push workflow files (needs a new PAT - but they haven't provided one in this message either) Add Lighthouse score thresholds to lighthouse-audit.yml (fail if Performance < 70 or Accessibility < 90) Create a new GitHub Actions workflow for auto-generating searchindex.json and chapters.json from markdown files Let me start on tasks 2 and 3 while noting that the PAT hasn't been provided yet. For task 2 - I need to add a step after the audit that checks the scores and fails if: Performance < 70 Accessibility < 90 For task 3, I need to set up a workflow that triggers on pushes to markdown files and runs a Python script to generate the JSON files from the markdown structure. First, I should examine the current lighthouse-audit.yml file and look at the existing chapters.json and searchindex.json to understand their format before building the generation script. Task 3 — สร้าง generate-site-data.yml
Command
cat > /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/generate-site-data.yml << 'YAML' # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # generate-site-data.yml # Auto-generates chapters.json and search_index.json from markdown source files. # # Source convention: # docs/chapters/chapter-{N}.md — one file per chapter, N = 1..55 # Frontmatter (optional): # --- # num: 1 # title: บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร # phase: 1 # mcp_tools: 3 # status: complete # --- # # Outputs (committed back to repo): # docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json — {num_str: html, ...} # docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json — [{num, text}, ...] # # Triggers: # - push: changes to docs/chapters/**/*.md # - workflow_dispatch: manual run, optionally override source/output paths # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── name: Generate Site Data on: push: branches: [main] paths: - "docs/chapters/**/*.md" - "docs/chapters/*.md" workflow_dispatch: inputs: chapters_path: description: "Path to chapter markdown files (relative to repo root)" required: false default: "docs/chapters" output_path: description: "Output directory for JSON files (relative to repo root)" required: false default: "docs/manufacturing-os-site" dry_run: description: "Dry run — generate but do not commit (true/false)" required: false default: "false" permissions: contents: write env: CHAPTERS_PATH: ${{ github.event.inputs.chapters_path || 'docs/chapters' }} OUTPUT_PATH: ${{ github.event.inputs.output_path || 'docs/manufacturing-os-site' }} DRY_RUN: ${{ github.event.inputs.dry_run || 'false' }} jobs: generate: name: Generate chapters.json & search_index.json runs-on: ubuntu-latest steps: # ── 1. Checkout ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Checkout repository uses: actions/checkout@v4 with: token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }} fetch-depth: 0 # ── 2. Set up Python ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Set up Python uses: actions/setup-python@v5 with: python-version: "3.12" - name: Install Python dependencies run: pip install markdown PyYAML # ── 3. Check source directory ──────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Check source directory id: check run: | CHAPTERS_DIR="${CHAPTERS_PATH}" if [ ! -d "$CHAPTERS_DIR" ]; then echo "::warning::Source directory '${CHAPTERS_DIR}' does not exist — creating with example file" mkdir -p "$CHAPTERS_DIR" # Create example chapter file so the directory is not empty cat > "${CHAPTERS_DIR}/chapter-01.md" << 'MD' --- num: 1 title: บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร phase: 1 mcp_tools: 3 status: complete --- # 1. บทสรุปสำหรับผู้บริหาร > เพิ่มเนื้อหา chapter ที่นี่ MD echo "created_example=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT fi COUNT=$(find "$CHAPTERS_DIR" -name "*.md" | wc -l) echo "Found ${COUNT} markdown file(s) in ${CHAPTERS_DIR}" echo "count=${COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT # ── 4. Generate JSON files ──────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Generate chapters.json and search_index.json id: generate run: | python3 - << 'PYEOF' import os, re, json, html as html_mod import markdown import yaml CHAPTERS_DIR = os.environ['CHAPTERS_PATH'] OUTPUT_DIR = os.environ['OUTPUT_PATH'] CHAPTERS_JSON = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'chapters.json') SEARCH_JSON = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'search_index.json') # ── Parse frontmatter ──────────────────────────────────────────────── FRONT_RE = re.compile(r'^---\s*\n(.*?)\n---\s*\n', re.DOTALL) def parse_file(path): with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as f: raw = f.read() meta = {} body = raw m = FRONT_RE.match(raw) if m: try: meta = yaml.safe_load(m.group(1)) or {} except Exception: meta = {} body = raw[m.end():] return meta, body # ── Infer chapter number from filename or frontmatter ──────────────── def get_num(meta, filename): if 'num' in meta: return int(meta['num']) # chapter-01.md, chapter-1.md, 01-intro.md, 1.md m = re.search(r'(\d+)', os.path.basename(filename)) return int(m.group(1)) if m else None # ── Convert markdown to HTML ───────────────────────────────────────── md = markdown.Markdown( extensions=['extra', 'toc', 'codehilite', 'nl2br'], extension_configs={ 'toc': {'permalink': True}, 'codehilite': {'guess_lang': False}, } ) # ── Collect and sort chapters ──────────────────────────────────────── files = sorted( [f for f in os.listdir(CHAPTERS_DIR) if f.endswith('.md')], key=lambda f: (get_num({}, f) or 9999, f) ) chapters_dict = {} search_entries = [] skipped = [] for fname in files: path = os.path.join(CHAPTERS_DIR, fname) meta, body = parse_file(path) num = get_num(meta, fname) if num is None: skipped.append(fname) continue md.reset() html_content = md.convert(body) # Add heading id anchor from chapter number if not present if not html_content.startswith('<h1'): title = meta.get('title', f'Chapter {num}') html_content = f'<h1 id="{num}">{num}. {title}</h1>\n' + html_content chapters_dict[str(num)] = html_content # Build plain-text search entry plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', html_content) plain = html_mod.unescape(plain) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() search_entries.append({'num': num, 'text': plain[:6000]}) search_entries.sort(key=lambda x: x['num']) # ── Write output files ─────────────────────────────────────────────── os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True) # Preserve existing chapters that have NO source .md file # (so we don't accidentally delete chapters built from other sources) existing = {} if os.path.exists(CHAPTERS_JSON): with open(CHAPTERS_JSON) as f: existing = json.load(f) new_nums = set(chapters_dict.keys()) merged = {**existing, **chapters_dict} # new files override existing # Re-sort by numeric key merged = dict(sorted(merged.items(), key=lambda x: int(x[0]))) with open(CHAPTERS_JSON, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(merged, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) # Regenerate full search index from merged chapters full_search = [] for num_str, html_content in merged.items(): plain = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', html_content) plain = html_mod.unescape(plain) plain = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', plain).strip() full_search.append({'num': int(num_str), 'text': plain[:6000]}) full_search.sort(key=lambda x: x['num']) with open(SEARCH_JSON, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(full_search, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) # ── Report ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── chap_kb = os.path.getsize(CHAPTERS_JSON) / 1024 search_kb = os.path.getsize(SEARCH_JSON) / 1024 print(f"✅ Generated from {len(files)} .md file(s)") print(f" New/updated chapters: {len(new_nums)}") if skipped: print(f" Skipped (no chapter number): {skipped}") print(f" Total chapters in index: {len(merged)}") print(f" chapters.json: {chap_kb:.1f} KB") print(f" search_index.json: {search_kb:.1f} KB") # Write outputs for subsequent steps with open(os.environ['GITHUB_OUTPUT'], 'a') as out: out.write(f"new_chapters={len(new_nums)}\n") out.write(f"total_chapters={len(merged)}\n") out.write(f"chapters_kb={chap_kb:.1f}\n") out.write(f"search_kb={search_kb:.1f}\n") PYEOF env: CHAPTERS_PATH: ${{ env.CHAPTERS_PATH }} OUTPUT_PATH: ${{ env.OUTPUT_PATH }} # ── 5. Validate output ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Validate generated files run: | CHAPTERS_FILE="${OUTPUT_PATH}/chapters.json" SEARCH_FILE="${OUTPUT_PATH}/search_index.json" for FILE in "$CHAPTERS_FILE" "$SEARCH_FILE"; do if [ ! -f "$FILE" ]; then echo "::error::Expected output file not found: $FILE" exit 1 fi # Validate JSON is parseable python3 -c "import json; data=json.load(open('${FILE}')); print(f' ✅ {\"${FILE}\".split(\"/\")[-1]}: valid JSON')" \ || { echo "::error::Invalid JSON in $FILE"; exit 1; } done CHAPTER_COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json; print(len(json.load(open('${CHAPTERS_FILE}'))))") SEARCH_COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json; print(len(json.load(open('${SEARCH_FILE}'))))") echo "chapters.json entries: ${CHAPTER_COUNT}" echo "search_index.json entries: ${SEARCH_COUNT}" if [ "$CHAPTER_COUNT" -lt 1 ]; then echo "::error::chapters.json is empty" exit 1 fi # ── 6. Commit and push ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Commit and push updated JSON files if: env.DRY_RUN != 'true' run: | git config user.name "github-actions[bot]" git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" git add \ "${OUTPUT_PATH}/chapters.json" \ "${OUTPUT_PATH}/search_index.json" if git diff --cached --quiet; then echo "No changes to JSON files — skipping commit" else NEW="${{ steps.generate.outputs.new_chapters }}" TOTAL="${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }}" git commit -m "chore(data): regenerate chapters.json + search_index.json [${NEW} chapters updated, ${TOTAL} total] [skip changelog]" git push echo "Pushed updated JSON files ✅" fi # ── 7. Dry run summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Dry run notice if: env.DRY_RUN == 'true' run: | echo "DRY RUN — files generated but NOT committed." echo " chapters.json: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.chapters_kb }} KB" echo " search_index.json: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.search_kb }} KB" echo " Total chapters: ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }}" # ── 8. Write job summary ───────────────────────────────────────────────── - name: Write job summary if: always() run: | cat >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY << SUMMARY ## 📦 Site Data Generation | File | Size | Chapters | |---|---|---| | \`chapters.json\` | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.chapters_kb }} KB | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }} | | \`search_index.json\` | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.search_kb }} KB | ${{ steps.generate.outputs.total_chapters }} | - **Source path:** \`${CHAPTERS_PATH}\` - **Output path:** \`${OUTPUT_PATH}\` - **New/updated chapters this run:** ${{ steps.generate.outputs.new_chapters }} - **Dry run:** ${DRY_RUN} SUMMARY YAML echo "Created: $(wc -l < /home/sandbox/monolith_site/.github/workflows/generate-site-data.yml) lines"

นี่คือ prompt สำหรับให้ Codex ตรวจสอบ repo และงานทั้งหมดครับ:

Codex Audit Prompt — Monolith Manufacturing OS
You are auditing the GitHub repository: indetailsgroup-hue/monolith-workspace

## TASK
Perform a full audit of the Monolith Manufacturing OS documentation system.
Check the actual files in the repo against the expected spec below.
Report PASS / WARN / FAIL for each item. Do not assume — read the actual files.

---

## 1. REPOSITORY STRUCTURE CHECK
Verify these paths exist in the repo (branch: main):

docs/
├── manufacturing-os-site/
│   ├── index.html
│   ├── style.css
│   ├── script.js
│   ├── chapters.json          ← must be valid JSON, key count >= 1
│   ├── search_index.json      ← must be valid JSON array, length >= 1
│   ├── analytics.html
│   ├── changelog.html         ← must contain <!-- COMMIT_LOG_INSERT_HERE -->
│   ├── status.html
│   └── README.md              ← must contain exactly 9 badge links
└── chapters/
    ├── chapter-01.md          ← through chapter-55.md (55 files total)
    └── ... (all 55 files)

.github/
└── workflows/
    ├── deploy-docs-pages.yml
    ├── deploy-docs-ci.yml
    ├── sync-chapters.yml
    ├── auto-update-changelog.yml
    ├── notify-slack-deploy.yml
    ├── lighthouse-audit.yml
    ├── generate-site-data.yml        ← must be v2 with caching (>= 400 lines)
    ├── auto-deploy-scispace.yml      ← NEW (>= 270 lines)
    └── validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml ← NEW (>= 300 lines)

---

## 2. WORKFLOW FILE SPEC CHECK

### generate-site-data.yml
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `force_rebuild` input
- Has `push` trigger on `docs/chapters/**/*.md`
- Contains SHA256 caching logic referencing `.github/chapter-cache.json`
- Contains `.github/chapter-hashes-pending.json` reference
- Has at least 8 distinct steps

### auto-deploy-scispace.yml
- Has `workflow_run` trigger watching "Generate Site Data"
- Has `push` trigger on paths:
  - docs/manufacturing-os-site/chapters.json
  - docs/manufacturing-os-site/search_index.json
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `force_deploy` input
- Has 2 jobs: `check-trigger` and `deploy`
- `deploy` job has `if: needs.check-trigger.outputs.should_deploy == 'true'`
- Uses `actions/configure-pages@v4`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3`, `actions/deploy-pages@v4`
- Has Slack curl notification step
- Has `concurrency.group: "pages"`

### validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml
- Has `push` and `pull_request` triggers on `docs/chapters/**/*.md`
- Has `workflow_dispatch` with `chapters_path` and `strict_mode` inputs
- Python script validates: num, title, phase, mcp_tools, status
- Checks types: num/phase/mcp_tools must be int
- Validates status enum: complete | in-progress | planned | draft
- Cross-checks filename number vs frontmatter `num`
- Uses `::error file=...::` annotation syntax
- Has PR comment step using `actions/github-script@v7`

### lighthouse-audit.yml
- Performance threshold >= 70 (fail if below)
- Accessibility threshold >= 90 (fail if below)
- `continue-on-error: true` on threshold check step
- Uploads HTML artifact (30 days retention)
- Posts/updates PR comment with scores

---

## 3. CHAPTER MARKDOWN FRONTMATTER SPOT CHECK
Read 5 chapter files: chapter-01.md, chapter-10.md, chapter-25.md, chapter-40.md, chapter-55.md
For each verify:
- Starts with `---`
- Contains fields: num, title, phase, mcp_tools, status
- `num` is integer matching filename number
- `status` is one of: complete, in-progress, planned, draft
- `phase` is integer 1–15

---

## 4. JSON DATA FILES CHECK (if chapters.json exists)
- Is valid JSON
- Top-level is an object with string keys "1" through "55"
- Each value is a non-empty string (HTML content)
- Total keys: report actual count

For search_index.json:
- Is valid JSON array
- Each entry has `num` (int) and `text` (string) fields
- Report total entry count

---

## 5. README BADGES CHECK (docs/manufacturing-os-site/README.md)
Count total badge lines (lines starting with [![)
Expected: 9 badges
List all badge names found.

---

## 6. RECENT COMMITS SUMMARY
List the last 5 commits on main:
- Hash (short)
- Message
- Files changed count

---

## 7. WORKFLOW RUNS STATUS (last 5)
For each of these workflows, report last run status:
- generate-site-data.yml
- auto-deploy-scispace.yml
- validate-chapter-frontmatter.yml
- deploy-docs-pages.yml
- lighthouse-audit.yml

---

## OUTPUT FORMAT
For each section, output:

### [Section Name]
| Item | Expected | Actual | Status |
|------|----------|--------|--------|
| ...  | ...      | ...    | ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / ❌ FAIL |

End with a summary:
## Overall Audit Summary
- Total checks: N
- Pass: N | Warn: N | Fail: N
- Critical issues (FAIL items): list them
- Recommended actions: list them

ดึง public URL bytes โดยตรง — ตรวจ headers และ hash จาก live CDN
Command
BASE="https://0ly1b489.scispace.co" TS=$(date -u +%s) echo "=== Fetch at $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) ===" echo "" for FILE in script.js chapters.json search_index.json index.html; do URL="${BASE}/${FILE}?nocache=${TS}" OUT="/tmp/live_${FILE//\//_}" # Fetch with no-cache headers, capture response headers HTTP=$(curl -fsSL \ -H "Cache-Control: no-cache, no-store" \ -H "Pragma: no-cache" \ -D "/tmp/live_headers_${FILE//\//_}.txt" \ -o "$OUT" -w "%{http_code}" \ "$URL") SIZE=$(wc -c < "$OUT") HASH=$(sha256sum "$OUT" | cut -d' ' -f1) # Extract key headers LAST_MOD=$(grep -i "last-modified" "/tmp/live_headers_${FILE//\//_}.txt" | tr -d '\r' | head -1) CACHE_STATUS=$(grep -i "x-cache\|x-amz-cf\|cf-cache\|age:" "/tmp/live_headers_${FILE//\//_}.txt" | tr -d '\r' | head -3 | tr '\n' '|') ETAG=$(grep -i "^etag" "/tmp/live_headers_${FILE//\//_}.txt" | tr -d '\r' | head -1) echo "── $FILE ──" echo " HTTP : $HTTP" echo " Size : $SIZE bytes" echo " SHA256 : $HASH" echo " $LAST_MOD" echo " $ETAG" echo " Cache : $CACHE_STATUS" echo "" done

Thought
Key findings: No Cache-Control header from origin - The S3 origin does NOT send a Cache-Control header. This means CloudFront uses its default TTL which is 86400 seconds (24 hours) when no Cache-Control is set. x-amz-server-side-encryption: AES256 - Hosted on S3 server: AmazonS3 - Direct S3 origin age: 118 - This HIO52-P5 PoP cached it 118 seconds ago (from the earlier fetch) The SciSpace deploywebsite tool also doesn't expose any cache invalidation controls, which compounds the issue — without a Cache-Control header, CloudFront defaults to caching for a full 24 hours, meaning old content could persist at edge nodes until roughly 24 hours after the last modification time, unless something else is triggering invalidation. Comparing HIO52-P5 (which got new content after a Miss then Hit within 118 seconds) against BKK51-P1 (still serving old content well after that), it looks like no invalidation was actually triggered by the deploy tool, or the invalidation hasn't propagated to BKK51-P1 yet. I should be upfront that the deploy tool I have access to doesn't expose any invalidation endpoint or parameter. Since BKK51-P1 cached the old content around 04:33Z, it could persist there until roughly the same time tomorrow unless something clears it. Now I'll put together the correction report.

Thought
The user is correcting my TTL estimate. I incorrectly stated: Default TTL = 86,400s (24h) — this is wrong because absence of Cache-Control header from origin does NOT reveal the CloudFront cache policy. CloudFront can be configured with a custom cache policy that overrides S3 origin headers. I used Last-Modified time as cache insertion time — that's wrong. Last-Modified is the S3 object modification time, not when the CloudFront edge cached it. I should: Accept PARTIAL status Acknowledge the correction clearly Record what IS known vs what is UNKNOWN No support message, no redeploy, no new URL Wait for further handoff
